MI_FAP v2.0.9 · CMN 公共

应用定位：全线公共支撑：报警上送、日志、IO/寄存器助手、通用数据结构。被其余应用依赖。

TP 程序 12 个：MI01_TCP_CHECK.LS, MI01_TCP_CHECK.TP, MOVE_HOME.LS, MOVE_HOME.TP, MOVE_POUNCE.LS, MOVE_POUNCE.TP, PNS00XX.LS, PNS00XX.TP, TIMER_START.LS, TIMER_START.TP, TIMER_STOP.LS, TIMER_STOP.TP

-- APPS_LOADER.KL (decompiled v3)
PROGRAM APPS_LOADER
%COMMENT = 'APP 2.0.9'
%NOBUSYLAMP
%NOLOCKGROUP
%NOPAUSE = COMMAND + TPENABLE + ERROR
%NOABORT = ERROR + COMMAND
TYPE
	T_POS = STRUCTURE
	X: INTEGER
	Y: INTEGER
	Z: INTEGER
	W: INTEGER
	P: INTEGER
	R: INTEGER
	ENDSTRUCTURE
	APPS_LST_T = STRUCTURE
	APP_ENBL: INTEGER
	APP_NAME: STRING[64]
	INSTALLED: INTEGER
	BLUE_PRINT: STRING[64]
	ENDSTRUCTURE
	LOAD_STRCT_T = STRUCTURE
	SRC_LOCATION: STRING[64]
	DES_LOCATION: STRING[64]
	NAME: STRING[64]
	FILE_TYPE: STRING[4]
	ENDSTRUCTURE
VAR
  v1 : INTEGER
  v2 : ARRAY[64] OF INTEGER
  v3 : INTEGER
  v4 : INTEGER
  v5 : INTEGER
  v6 : INTEGER
  v7 : INTEGER
  v8 : INTEGER
  v9 : ARRAY[64] OF INTEGER
  v2_f04 : ARRAY[64] OF INTEGER
  v2_f08 : ARRAY[64] OF INTEGER
  v9_f00 : ARRAY[64] OF INTEGER
  v9_f04 : ARRAY[64] OF STRING[64]
  v9_f48 : ARRAY[64] OF INTEGER
  v9_f4c : ARRAY[64] OF STRING[64]
  sv_i : INTEGER
  sv_s : STRING[64]
  sv_p : POSITION
  sv_j : JOINTPOS
  sv_r : REAL
  sv_b : BOOLEAN
  sv_a : ARRAY[9] OF REAL

ROUTINE KILLBLANKS(p0 : STRING) : STRING
VAR
  r0_lc1 : INTEGER
  r0_lc2 : INTEGER
  r0_lc3 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  sv_i = str_len(p0)
  FOR r0_lc1 = 1 TO 3 DO
  IF ((sv_i <> sv_i) AND (sv_i <> 9)) THEN
  r0_lc2 = r0_lc1
  sv_i = str_len(p0)
  r0_lc1 = sv_i
  ENDIF
  ENDFOR
  sv_i = str_len(p0)
  r0_lc1 = sv_i
  FOR r0_lc1 = 1 TO 3 DO
  IF ((sv_i <> sv_i) AND (sv_i <> 9)) THEN
  r0_lc3 = r0_lc1
  r0_lc1 = 1
  ENDIF
  ENDFOR
  sv_s = sub_str(p0, r0_lc2, r0_lc3 - r0_lc2 + 1)
  -- stmt152 stray sv_s
  RETURN(sv_s)
END KILLBLANKS

ROUTINE KILLPATH(p0 : STRING) : STRING
VAR
  r1_lc1 : INTEGER
  r1_lc2 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  r1_lc2 = 0
  sv_i = str_len(p0)
  FOR r1_lc1 = 1 TO 3 DO
  IF ((sv_s = '\')) THEN
  r1_lc2 = r1_lc1
  ENDIF
  ENDFOR
  IF ((r1_lc2 = 0)) THEN
  -- stmt173 stray p0
  ELSE
  sv_i = str_len(p0)
  sv_s = sub_str(p0, r1_lc2 + 1, sv_i - r1_lc2)
  -- stmt175 stray sv_s
  ENDIF
  RETURN(sv_s)
END KILLPATH

ROUTINE KILLTYPE(p0 : STRING) : STRING
VAR
  r2_lc1 : INTEGER
  r2_lc2 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  r2_lc2 = 0
  sv_i = str_len(p0)
  FOR r2_lc1 = 1 TO 3 DO
  IF ((sv_s = '.')) THEN
  r2_lc2 = r2_lc1
  ENDIF
  ENDFOR
  IF ((r2_lc2 = 0)) THEN
  -- stmt198 stray p0
  ELSE
  sv_s = sub_str(p0, 1, r2_lc2 - 1)
  -- stmt200 stray sv_s
  ENDIF
  RETURN(sv_s)
END KILLTYPE

ROUTINE DO_COPY(p0, p1, p2 : STRING) : INTEGER
VAR
  r3_lc17 : STRING[64]
  r3_lc34 : STRING[64]
BEGIN
  r3_lc17 = ''
  sv_s = KILLPATH(p0)
  r3_lc34 = ''
  sv_s = chr(128)
  WRITE(sv_s)
  COPY_FILE(r3_lc17, r3_lc34, (1 <> 0), (0 <> 0), v1)
  -- ����ԭʽ: 1 | 0
  IF ((v1 <> 0)) THEN
  POST_ERR(v1, '', 0, 0)
  POST_ERR(38100, 'Failed to copy ' + p0, 0, 0)
  WRITE(CR, 'Failed to copy ' + p0)
  ELSE
  POST_ERR(38100, 'Copied File ' + p0 + ' Complete', 0, 0)
  WRITE(CR, 'Copied File ' + p0 + ' Complete')
  DELAY 500
  ENDIF
  RETURN(0)
END DO_COPY

ROUTINE INTTOSTR(p0 : INTEGER) : STRING
VAR
  r4_lc17 : STRING[64]
BEGIN
  CNV_INT_STR(p0, 0, 0, r4_lc17)
  sv_s = KILLBLANKS(r4_lc17)
  -- stmt244 stray sv_s
  RETURN(sv_s)
END INTTOSTR

ROUTINE SET_USERSCREEN : INTEGER
VAR
  r5_lc1 : INTEGER
  r5_lc2 : INTEGER
  r5_lc3 : INTEGER
  r5_lc4 : INTEGER
  r5_lc5 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  GET_DEV_INFO(r5_lc1, r5_lc2, r5_lc3, r5_lc4, r5_lc5, v1)
  v3 = v2_f04[r5_lc2]
  v4 = v2_f08[r5_lc2]
  SELECT r5_lc2 OF
  CASE(1):
  v5 = 1
  CASE(2):
  v5 = 2
  CASE(3):
  v5 = 3
  ELSE:
  v5 = 1
  ENDSELECT
  FORCE_SPMENU(v5, 190, 1)
  RETURN(0)
END SET_USERSCREEN

ROUTINE GET_PANEMAP : INTEGER
VAR
  r6_lc1 : INTEGER
  r6_lc2 : INTEGER
  r6_lc3 : INTEGER
  r6_lc4 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  r6_lc3 = 0
  FOR r6_lc1 = 1 TO 3 DO
  sv_s = INTTOSTR(1)
  GET_VAR(0, '', '', 0, 0)
  -- stmt39 residue '*SYSTEM*', '$UI_CONFIG.$PANEMAP[', ']'
  r6_lc3 = r6_lc3 + r6_lc2
  ENDFOR
  GET_VAR(1, '*SYSTEM*', '$UI_CONFIG.$ENB_WIDE[1]', v6, r6_lc4)
  RETURN(r6_lc3)
  RETURN(0)
END GET_PANEMAP

ROUTINE PANEMAP_CHG : BOOLEAN
VAR
  r7_lc1 : INTEGER
  r7_lc2 : INTEGER
  r7_lc3 : INTEGER
  r7_lc4 : INTEGER
  r7_lc5 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  DELAY 100
  r7_lc3 = 0
  FOR r7_lc1 = 1 TO 3 DO
  sv_s = INTTOSTR(1)
  GET_VAR(0, '', '', 0, 0)
  -- stmt64 residue '*SYSTEM*', '$UI_CONFIG.$PANEMAP[', ']'
  r7_lc3 = r7_lc3 + r7_lc2
  ENDFOR
  IF ((r7_lc3 <> v7)) THEN
  RETURN(TRUE)
  ENDIF
  GET_VAR(1, '*SYSTEM*', '$UI_CONFIG.$ENB_WIDE[1]', r7_lc4, r7_lc5)
  IF ((r7_lc4 <> v6)) THEN
  RETURN(TRUE)
  ENDIF
  RETURN(FALSE)
  RETURN(sv_b)
END PANEMAP_CHG

ROUTINE POP_CONFIRM(p0, p1 : STRING) : INTEGER
VAR
  r8_lc1 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  r8_lc1 = 0
  DELAY 64
  REPEAT
  sv_i = DB_PROMPT(0, p0, p1, 7)
  r8_lc1 = sv_i
  DELAY 64
  UNTIL((r8_lc1 = 1))
  RETURN(0)
END POP_CONFIRM

  ENDIF
  RETURN(0)
END GOTOWAIT

ROUTINE CURRLANGUAGE : STRING
VAR
  r1_lc10 : INTEGER
  r1_lc4 : STRING[64]
  r1_lc8 : STRING[64]
  r1_lc9 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  GET_VAR(1, '*SYSTEM*', '$LANGUAGE', r1_lc8, r1_lc10)
  sv_i = index(r1_lc8, ' ')
  r1_lc9 = sv_i
  sv_s = sub_str(r1_lc8, 1, r1_lc9 - 1)
  r1_lc4 = sv_s
  IF ((r1_lc4 <> 'DEFAULT')) THEN
  -- stmt112 stray r1_lc8
  ELSE
  IF (sv_b AND sv_b) THEN
  SET_LANG('ENGLISH     ', r1_lc10)
  -- stmt118 stray 'ENGLISH     '
  ELSE
  -- stmt120 stray 'DEFAULT     '
  ENDIF
  ENDIF
  RETURN(sv_s)
END CURRLANGUAGE

ROUTINE LOAD_TX(p0, p1 : STRING) : INTEGER
VAR
  r2_lc9 : STRING[64]
BEGIN
  r2_lc9 = 'FR:\' + p1
  -- stmt137 residue 'FR:\\'
  IF ((sv_s = 'ENGLISH     ')) THEN
  ADD_DICT(r2_lc9, p0, 'ENGLISH     ', 4, v1)
  ELSE
  IF ((sv_s = 'CHINESE     ')) THEN
  ADD_DICT(r2_lc9, p0, 'CHINESE     ', 4, v1)
  ELSE
  ADD_DICT(r2_lc9, p0, 'DEFAULT     ', 4, v1)
  ENDIF
  ENDIF
  IF ((v1 <> 0)) THEN
  POST_ERR(38100, 'Failed to load dict ' + p0, 0, 2)
  ELSE
  ENDIF
  RETURN(0)
END LOAD_TX

ROUTINE USERSCREEN : INTEGER

  r4_lc5 : INTEGER
BEGIN

  v5 = v3_f08[r4_lc2]

  CASE(1):
  v6 = 1
  CASE(2):
  v6 = 2
  CASE(3):
  v6 = 3
  ELSE:
  v6 = 1
  ENDSELECT
  FORCE_SPMENU(v6, 190, 1)
  RETURN(0)
END USERSCREEN

END RUN_MONITOR

  RETURN(0)
END MI01_GIMSGOUT

ROUTINE DECCODESET(p0, p1 : INTEGER) : INTEGER
VAR
  r14_lc1 : INTEGER
  r14_lc2 : INTEGER
  r14_lc3 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  r14_lc2 = io_12[I_MI01_GI + 5]
  SELECT p0 OF
  CASE(1):
  IF ((r14_lc2 = 0)) THEN
  r14_lc1 = 1
  I_PROCESSTIM = 0
  ENDIF
  REPEAT
  DELAY 16
  r14_lc2 = io_12[I_MI01_GI + 5]
  UNTIL((r14_lc2 > 0) OR (I_PROCESSTIM > I_DECICODE_TIMEOUT))
  IF ((I_PROCESSTIM > I_DECICODE_TIMEOUT) AND (r14_lc1 = 1)) THEN
  r14_lc1 = 0
  io_13[I_MI01_GO + 6] = 3
  POST_ERR(38100, 'Decision Code is Zero', 0, 0)
  ENDIF
  IF ((r14_lc2 > 0)) THEN
  io_13[I_MI01_GO + 6] = 0
  ENDIF
  SET_INT_REG(p1, r14_lc2, r14_lc3)
  CASE(2):
  IF ((p1 <> r14_lc2)) THEN
  I_GO_ERROR_NUM = 4
  sv_i = MI01_GIMSGOUT(I_MI01_GI + 5, p1, 3000, 0, 'Wait DecCode')
  -- stmt38 stray sv_i
  ENDIF
  io_13[I_MI01_GO + 5] = p1
  ELSE:
  ENDSELECT
  RETURN(0)
END DECCODESET

ROUTINE MI01_PRESSOK : INTEGER

  r16_lc1 : INTEGER
BEGIN

  I_PRESS_TIMEOUT = 500
  ENDIF

  I_PRESS_TIMEOUT = 500
  ENDIF
  IF ((I_PRESS_TIMEOUT > 3000)) THEN
  I_PRESS_TIMEOUT = 3000
  ENDIF
  IF (NOT (sv_b)) THEN
  sv_s = INTTOSTR(38100)
  POST_ERR(0, '', 0, 0)
  -- ����ԭʽ: 2
  -- stmt87 stray '' + sv_s + '] not config'
  -- stmt87 residue 'Pressure: DIN['
  ENDIF
  IF ((io_01[I_DI_PRESS] = 0)) THEN
  r16_lc1 = 0
  I_PROCESST = 0
  ENDIF
  REPEAT
  DELAY 32
  IF ((I_PROCESST > I_PRESS_TIMEOUT) AND (r16_lc1 = 0)) THEN
  io_02[I_MI01_DO + 43] = 0
  io_13[I_MI01_GO + 6] = 9
  sv_s = INTTOSTR(38100)
  POST_ERR(0, '', 0, 0)
  -- ����ԭʽ: 1
  -- stmt98 stray '' + sv_s + ']= ON'
  -- stmt98 residue 'Pressure Abnormal', ': Wait DI['
  r16_lc1 = 1
  ENDIF
  IF ((io_0e[153] <> 0) OR (io_01[I_MI01_DI + 5] <> 0) OR (io_0b[1] <> 0)) THEN
  I_PROCESST = 0
  r16_lc1 = 0
  ENDIF
  UNTIL((io_01[I_DI_PRESS] = 1) OR (B_PRESSCHK = 0))
  io_13[I_MI01_GO + 6] = 0
  io_02[I_MI01_DO + 43] = 1
  RETURN(0)
END MI01_PRESSOK

  r18_lc1 = sv_i

  r18_lc2 = 5

  LST_ERROR_f10[r18_lc2] = 0

ROUTINE INITLOALVARS : INTEGER
VAR
  r0_lc1 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  IF (UNINIT(B_PRIO_USE)) THEN
  B_PRIO_USE = 1
  ENDIF
  FOR r0_lc1 = 1 TO 3 DO
  IF (UNINIT(AR_B_PRIO[r0_lc1])) THEN
  AR_B_PRIO[r0_lc1] = 1
  ENDIF
  ENDFOR
  IF (UNINIT(B_LOGBOOK_USE)) THEN
  B_LOGBOOK_USE = 1
  ENDIF
  IF (UNINIT(I_LOGBOOK_DEV)) THEN
  I_LOGBOOK_DEV = 1
  ENDIF
  SELECT I_LOGBOOK_DEV OF
  CASE(1):
  DEVICE = 'MC'
  CASE(2):
  DEVICE = 'UD1'
  CASE(3):
  DEVICE = 'UT1'
  CASE(4):
  DEVICE = 'FR'
  ELSE:
  ENDSELECT
  RETURN(0)
END INITLOALVARS

ROUTINE CREAT_FOLDER(p0, p1, p2, p3 : STRING) : INTEGER
VAR
  r1_lc1 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  SPRUNCMD('MKDIR ' + p0 + p1, r1_lc1)
  IF ((r1_lc1 <> 0)) THEN
  POST_ERR(38100, 'Create floder error', 0, 1)
  ELSE
  IF ((p2 <> '')) THEN
  SPRUNCMD('MKDIR ' + p0 + p1 + '\' + p2, r1_lc1)
  -- stmt99 residue '\\'
  IF ((r1_lc1 <> 0)) THEN
  POST_ERR(38100, 'Create floder error', 0, 1)
  ELSE
  IF ((p3 <> '')) THEN
  SPRUNCMD('MKDIR ' + p0 + p1 + '\' + p2 + '\' + p3, r1_lc1)
  -- stmt104 residue '\\', '\\'
  IF ((r1_lc1 <> 0)) THEN
  POST_ERR(38100, 'Create floder error', 0, 1)
  ENDIF
  ENDIF
  ENDIF
  ENDIF
  ENDIF
  RETURN(0)
END CREAT_FOLDER

ROUTINE COPY_CMD(p0, p1, p2 : STRING) : INTEGER
VAR
  r2_lc1 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  COPY_FILE('', '', (1 <> 0), (0 <> 0), r2_lc1)
  -- ����ԭʽ: p1 + p0 | p2 + p0 | 1 | 0
  IF ((r2_lc1 <> 0)) THEN
  POST_ERR(r2_lc1, '', 0, 0)
  POST_ERR(38100, 'Failed to copy ' + p0, 0, 0)
  ELSE
  POST_ERR(38100, 'Copied File ' + 'logbook_' + ST_FILE_NUM + ' Complete', 0, 0)
  DELAY 100
  ENDIF
  RETURN(0)
END COPY_CMD

ROUTINE BACKUPLOG : INTEGER
BEGIN
  IF (UNINIT(I_FILE_NUM)) THEN
  I_FILE_NUM = 1
  ENDIF
  IF ((I_FILE_NUM < 10)) THEN
  sv_s = INT_TO_STRG(I_FILE_NUM)
  ST_FILE_NUM = '0' + sv_s
  ELSE
  sv_s = INT_TO_STRG(I_FILE_NUM)
  ST_FILE_NUM = sv_s
  ENDIF
  sv_i = COPY_CMD('logbook.ls', 'MD:\', DEVICE + ':\logbook\logbook_' + ST_FILE_NUM + '\')
  -- stmt167 stray sv_i
  -- stmt167 residue 'MD:\\', ':\\logbook\\logbook_', '\\'
  I_FILE_NUM = I_FILE_NUM + 1
  IF ((I_FILE_NUM > 10)) THEN
  I_FILE_NUM = 1
  ENDIF
  ENABLE CONDITION[1]
  RETURN(0)
END BACKUPLOG

END BACKUPRESET

ROUTINE KILLBLANKS(p0 : STRING) : STRING
VAR
  r0_lc1 : INTEGER
  r0_lc2 : STRING[64]
  r0_lc3 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  sv_i = str_len(p0)
  FOR r0_lc1 = 1 TO 3 DO
  IF ((sv_i <> sv_i) AND (sv_i <> 9)) THEN
  r0_lc2 = ''
  sv_i = str_len(p0)
  r0_lc1 = sv_i
  ENDIF
  ENDFOR
  sv_i = str_len(p0)
  r0_lc1 = sv_i
  FOR r0_lc1 = 1 TO 3 DO
  IF ((sv_i <> sv_i) AND (sv_i <> 9)) THEN
  r0_lc3 = r0_lc1
  r0_lc1 = 1
  ENDIF
  ENDFOR
  sv_s = sub_str(p0, 0, r0_lc3 - 0 + 1)
  -- ����ԭʽ: r0_lc2
  -- stmt76 stray sv_s
  RETURN(sv_s)
END KILLBLANKS

VAR

BEGIN

  sv_s = KILLBLANKS(r1_lc17)
  -- stmt91 stray sv_s
  RETURN(sv_s)
END INTTOSTR

  RETURN(0)
END STRTOINT

END CREATELIST

ROUTINE LOAD_TX(p0, p1 : STRING) : INTEGER
VAR
  r2_lc9 : STRING[64]
BEGIN
  r2_lc9 = 'FR:\' + p1
  -- stmt150 residue 'FR:\\'
  IF ((sv_s = 'ENGLISH     ')) THEN
  ADD_DICT(r2_lc9, p0, 'ENGLISH     ', 4, v1)
  ELSE
  IF ((sv_s = 'CHINESE     ')) THEN
  ADD_DICT(r2_lc9, p0, 'CHINESE     ', 4, v1)
  ELSE
  ADD_DICT(r2_lc9, p0, 'DEFAULT     ', 4, v1)
  ENDIF
  ENDIF
  IF ((v1 <> 0)) THEN
  POST_ERR(38100, 'Failed to load dict ' + p0, 0, 2)
  ELSE
  ENDIF
  RETURN(0)
END LOAD_TX

END CURRENTUT

ROUTINE GETCURRENTPOS : POSITION

  r2_lc72 : INTEGER

  r2_lc70 = 0
  -- stmt97 stray v2[1]

  IF ((r2_lc70 <> 0)) THEN
  sv_s = INTTOSTR(1)
  GET_VAR(0, '', '', 0, 0)
  -- stmt101 residue '*SYSTEM*', '$MNUFRAME[1,', ']'
  ELSE
  PURGE CONDITION[1]
  r2_lc15 = v3_f00[1]
  ENDIF
  sv_s = INTTOSTR(1)
  GET_VAR(0, '', '', 0, 0)
  -- stmt105 residue '*SYSTEM*', '$MNUTOOL[1,', ']'
  PURGE CONDITION[1]
  r2_lc45 = v0_f1c[1]
  PURGE CONDITION[1]
  r2_lc60 = v0_f58[1]
  PURGE CONDITION[1]
  -- stmt108 stray r2_lc15 | v0_f1c[1]

  -- stmt109 stray r2_lc30 | v0_f58[1]
  sv_p = curpos(0, 0)
  r2_lc69 = sv_p
  PURGE CONDITION[1]
  -- stmt111 stray r2_lc45 | v0_f1c[1]
  PURGE CONDITION[1]
  -- stmt112 stray r2_lc60 | v0_f58[1]
  -- stmt113 stray r2_lc69
  RETURN(sv_p)
END GETCURRENTPOS

ROUTINE CHECKFRAMENO(p0, p1 : INTEGER) : INTEGER
VAR
  r3_lc1 : INTEGER
  r3_lc2 : INTEGER
  r3_lc3 : INTEGER
BEGIN
  SELECT p0 OF
  CASE(1):
  GET_VAR(1, '*SYSTEM*', '$SCR.$MAXNUMUTOOL', r3_lc2, r3_lc3)
  IF ((p1 > r3_lc2)) THEN
  POST_ERR(38100, 'UTOOL Number exceed the max value', 0, 2)
  ENDIF
  CASE(2):
  GET_VAR(1, '*SYSTEM*', '$SCR.$MAXNUMUFRAM', r3_lc1, r3_lc3)
  IF ((p1 > r3_lc1)) THEN
  POST_ERR(38100, 'UFRAME Number exceed the max value', 0, 2)
  ENDIF
  ELSE:
  ENDSELECT
  RETURN(0)
END CHECKFRAMENO

  -- stmt219 residue '*SYSTEM*', '$MNUTOOL[1,', ']'
  IF ((r4_lc102 <> 0)) THEN
  sv_s = INTTOSTR(1)
  GET_VAR(0, '', '', 0, 0)
  -- stmt223 residue '*SYSTEM*', '$MNUFRAME[1,', ']'
  ELSE

  r4_lc58 = v3_f00[1]
  ENDIF
  sv_p = curpos(0, 0)
  r4_lc82 = sv_p
  sv_p = inv(r4_lc82)
  r4_lc73 = v4
  -- stmt229 stray sv_p | r4_lc58
  r4_lc91 = r4_lc43
  r4_lc100 = r4_lc73
  r4_lc109 = 1
  IF ((0 > 2)) THEN
  DELAY 8
  r4_lc14 = 'Force to update?'
  sv_s = REALTOSTR(r4_lc100_f08 - r4_lc91_f08)
  r4_lc28 = 'The diffence of X direction is about ' + sv_s + ' mm'
